Generated by `node tools/build-people.mjs`. These 2759 of 2950 people have at least one date that the Bible does not record. The placeholder is estimated from their generation or the period in which they appear. Fix a date in `data/characters.js` (key figures) or `data/source/insight-people.json` (everyone else), then rebuild.
